- Removed the `<AttendeeLookupCard>` item from `refactor-opportunities.md`'s "Missing shared components" list without building it. Unlike the other four items in that list (all had genuine, verified duplicated JSX to consolidate), this one didn't hold up on inspection: `BidEntry.tsx`'s "lookup preview" is just two plain `<p>` tags with no card/badge markup, and `Bidders.tsx`'s attendee info (`getBidderAttendeeInfo`) is inline badge logic embedded in the main table's row cells, not a separate duplicated lookup-card pattern. Building a new card component here would have been net-new UI design work, not a dedup - a real decision point, taken to the user rather than assumed; the answer was to skip it and correct the backlog instead.
- Reconciled the two disagreeing Bid-Management column-pair grid parsers flagged in `refactor-opportunities.md` (`useCheckoutState.ts`'s `loadWinners` vs. `useWinnersData.ts`'s `fetchWinners`), which could disagree about which items counted as "won" on a large auction due to different fetch ranges (`!1:100` vs. the effectively-unbounded `!A:ZZ`, while `server/services/auctionService.ts`'s `recordWinningBid` writes bids scanning up to row 300) and different cell guards (`row[j] && row[j+1]` vs. `row[j]` alone). Extracted the shared parse into a new `src/lib/bidGridParsing.ts` (`parseBidGrid`), standardized on `useWinnersData.ts`'s wider range and looser guard - the load-bearing one, since `Items.tsx:70-71` already relies on an item with a recorded winner but a blank/malformed price cell still counting as won. `useWinnersData.ts` was a pure refactor onto the new shared function (its original range/guard already matched); `useCheckoutState.ts`'s `loadWinners` is the one with an actual behavior change - it now groups `parseBidGrid`'s flat output by bidder instead of hand-rolling the same column-pair double loop, and picks up the wider range and looser guard in the process. Added `src/lib/bidGridParsing.test.ts` (8 tests: basic parse, multiple wins per bidder, blank/malformed price defaulting to 0 with a comment explaining why per `Items.tsx`'s reliance on it, skipping a column with no bidder ID, skipping a row with no item ID, currency-formatted price parsing, undefined/null/too-short input, and reading well past row 100 as a regression test named after the old `useCheckoutState.ts` cap). Removed the now-resolved finding from `refactor-opportunities.md` and narrowed its related test-coverage-gap entry for `useWinnersData.ts` to the parts that remain genuinely untested (the bidder-name lookup and `recordWinningBid`'s optimistic-update/rollback path). Test count: 369 → 377 (97 → 105 frontend); updated the count everywhere it's asserted (`AGENTS.md`, `docs/agents/README.md`, `docs/agents/testing-batches.md`). `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 377/377, `npm run build` clean.
- Moved all 26 existing `.gs` files out of `scripts/GoogleAppsScriptsCode/` and into a new `scripts/GoogleAppsScriptsCode/BidManagementSpreadsheet/` subfolder (via `git mv`, preserving history), per the user's plan to add the Item-Entry and Tickets spreadsheets' Apps Script code into this repo as sibling subfolders (`ItemsSpreadsheet/`, `TicketsSpreadsheet/`, both created empty, pending the user copying real source in) rather than merging all three spreadsheets' scripts into one flat, ambiguous directory. Updated the three Node `vm`-harness test files that load real `.gs` source by path (`server/test/gs-google-ai-studio.test.ts`, `server/test/gs-paypal-service.test.ts`, `server/test/gs-paypal-sweeper.test.ts`) to the new `BidManagementSpreadsheet/` paths, and the one live-reference-copy pointer in `refactor-opportunities.md` (the dead `AuctionItem`/`Bidder` data-model classes finding). Pure file move - no `.gs` source content changed, so nothing needs re-copying into the live Apps Script editor. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 377/377 (44 of them the real-source GAS harness tests, re-run directly against the new paths to confirm the loader still resolves them), `npm run build` clean.

### Fixed

- Missing `aria-label` on `AuctionItemsTab.tsx`'s icon-only delete button (now `AuctionItemRow.tsx`).
- Manual template-literal `className` for row-selection styling in `AuctionItemsTab.tsx` replaced with `cn()` (now `AuctionItemRow.tsx`).
- A `colSpan={6}` on `AuctionItemsTab.tsx`'s 8-column table's empty-state row, corrected to `colSpan={8}` (now `AuctionItemsTable.tsx`).
- Removed dead `Bidder`/`Attendee`/`Guest` imports and a no-op empty `useEffect` from `AuctionItemsTab.tsx`.
- **Tickets-spreadsheet hardening: fixed a high-severity, real-world bidder-number bug affecting the printed bidder labels handed out at the auction.** `TicketsSpreadsheet/Class_BidderManager.gs`'s `getAllBidders()` had two compounding problems, cross-checked against `src/lib/attendeeParsing.ts` and `server/services/syncService.ts` (the Bid-Management app's authoritative sync, `performSheetSync()`). (1) A column-index bug: on the "tickets" sheet, Guest 2 and Guest 3's name/email columns used the unshifted offsets (`10/11`, `13/14`) instead of the correct ones (`11/12/13`, `15/16/17`) - the shift is caused by an "own bidder number" flag column inserted after Guest 1, and `attendeeParsing.ts`/`syncService.ts` already handle it correctly; this class didn't, producing garbled names on physical labels. (2) A far more serious architectural bug: this class *invented* sequential bidder numbers locally (`this.baseBidderNumber = 101; nextNumber++`) instead of reading the app's real, already-assigned numbers - completely disconnected from the actual source of truth (`Bidder-Numbers`, written by `performSheetSync()`/`BidManagementSpreadsheet/Class_BidderManager.gs`'s `copyBiddersToBidderSheet()`), in a different interleaved order, and without honoring the "opted in for their own number" flag guests must set. Since printed labels are physically affixed to bidding guides before the auction and their numbers are what bidders actually use to bid, a label with the wrong number risked someone bidding - and winning - under a number that wasn't really theirs. Per the user's explicit workflow confirmation (numbers are always synced before labels are printed in real use) and choices (pass the Bid-Management spreadsheet ID from the Node app; hard-error rather than silently mis-printing), rewrote `getAllBidders(bidManagementSpreadsheetId)` to open the Bid-Management spreadsheet, build a `{normalizedKey: id}` map from its real `Bidder-Numbers` sheet (`matchKey_` replicates `syncService.ts`'s `formatName`/hash logic byte-for-byte: capitalize-first/lowercase-rest each name part, lowercase-trim email, joined with `|`), look up every primary/guest bidder's real ID instead of generating one (using the corrected guest column triples), throw a hard error naming any unmatched **primary** bidder (a real gap - sync hasn't run for them), silently skip an unmatched **guest** (the normal case - not everyone opts in), and return the list sorted ascending by real bidder number. Added the `overrides = {}` injectable-dependency pattern (plus the same guarded `getUi()`-in-API-context fix already applied to the other two spreadsheets) to `TicketsSpreadsheet/Class_GlobalVariables.gs`, and a `bidManagementSpreadsheet` override to `BidderManager` itself so tests can skip the real `SpreadsheetApp.openById` call. Restructured `Class_BidderLabelsDoc.gs`'s `BidderLabelsDoc` constructor to accept an already-fetched, already-validated `bidders` array instead of a lazy `bidderManager` reference - `getAllBidders()` now runs (and can throw) *before* the Avery-template Drive copy is made, so a hard error never leaves an orphaned empty label doc behind; updated its menu-only `generateBidderLabels()` wrapper to interactively prompt for the Bid-Management spreadsheet ID via `ui.prompt(...)` rather than deleting it (the Tickets menu-disabling question hasn't been asked/decided yet, unlike the other two spreadsheets). Updated `Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs`'s `createBidderLabels(folderID, bidManagementSpreadsheetId)` to fetch/validate bidders first, and added an injectable `bidderManager` override to its constructor. **Also fixes a second, previously-unreported bug as a direct consequence**: `server/routes/productionRoutes.ts`'s `/tickets/bidder-labels` route was passing `apiCreateBidderLabels` an empty parameter list (`[]`) - unlike every Items-spreadsheet endpoint, which passes `[folderId || ""]` - so the generated label doc's `folderID` was always `undefined`, silently falling back to `_Settings`'s `auctionDevFolderID` (the developer's old working folder) instead of the intended For-Print folder; the route now forwards `[folderId || "", bidManagementSpreadsheetId || ""]`, and `TicketDocumentCreationTab.tsx` now sends both from `ConfigContext` (`forPrintFolderId`, `bidManagementSpreadsheetId`) in the request body, with a new pre-flight guard toast if the Bid Management spreadsheet ID isn't configured. Added `server/test/gs-tickets-bidder-manager.test.ts` (9 tests) and `server/test/gs-tickets-google-ai-studio.test.ts` (6 tests) on the Node side via `loadGasClasses`, plus the matching native tier `TicketsSpreadsheet/BidderManager_UnitTests.gs` (`runTicketsSpreadsheetUnitTests`, 11 tests); added `src/components/TicketDocumentCreationTab.test.tsx` (5 tests - this file had zero coverage before). Updated `server/test/production.test.ts`'s shared parametrized happy-path test to assert the new two-parameter contract (`expectedParams`) instead of the old param-less one. Every fix sanity-checked against a deliberately reintroduced bug before being trusted: reintroduced the original wrong guest-column offsets and confirmed the corrected-columns test failed, then reverted; confirmed the whole `TicketsSpreadsheet/` folder still loads cleanly together in dependency order via the Node `vm` sandbox. Test count: 482 → 501 (294 → 308 backend, 188 → 193 frontend, plus 11 native-tier GAS tests not counted in either); updated the count everywhere it's asserted. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 501/501, `npm run build` clean. **Reference-copy work only - `Class_GlobalVariables.gs`, `Class_BidderManager.gs`, `Class_BidderLabelsDoc.gs`, `Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs`, and the new `BidderManager_UnitTests.gs` all need to be manually copied into the live Tickets Apps Script editor before this fix (or the native tests) take effect there.**

- **Continued Tickets-spreadsheet hardening: closed a live footgun where re-running "Set Up Bid Management App" after any walk-in bidder had already been registered could silently reassign or duplicate that person's bidder number.** `server/services/syncService.ts`'s `performSheetSync()` does a full rebuild of `Bidder-Numbers` on every run (rename-to-backup, then regenerate from scratch from the ticket sheets) - it's not additive. `server/services/bidderService.ts`'s `registerWalkInBidder()` (the day-of "someone walks in without a ticket" flow) appends a row directly to both `Bidder-Numbers` (with its own locally-computed ID) and the live Tickets spreadsheet's `tickets` sheet, and the "Set Up Bid Management App" button (`App.tsx`) has no confirmation dialog and stays clickable any time the auction isn't closed - including mid-auction, after walk-ins already have real bids recorded against their assigned ID in `Bid-Management`. Re-clicking it at that point would sweep the walk-in back up as an ordinary "ticket sheet" bidder and hand them a new sequential ID, orphaning whatever was already recorded under the old one - exactly the kind of drift that could let someone win an item under a number that isn't really theirs, the same integrity concern behind the bidder-label fix above. Per the user's explicit choice (block re-sync outright once any walk-in exists, rather than just a confirmation dialog or a much larger rework of sync to be additive), added a guard to the front of `performSheetSync()`: it reads the `tickets` sheet's payment-method column (T, index 19) for the `"Auction Checkout"` marker `registerWalkInBidder()` already writes there, and - before any backup/rename/rebuild work begins - hard-errors naming every walk-in found, so staff get a clear, specific reason instead of silent corruption. Also fixed a real wiring gap surfaced while testing this: `dataRoutes.ts`'s `/bidders/sync` route let a thrown sync error fall through to the app's generic `errorHandler`, which responds `{error: "Internal Server Error", message: err.message}` - but `App.tsx`'s `handleSyncBidders` reads `result.error`, not `.message`, so the real reason (including the walk-in's name) would never have reached the toast the user actually sees; it would've just said "Internal Server Error." Fixed by having the route catch and re-surface both this new guard's error and the pre-existing missing-settings error as a 400 with the real message, matching the pattern already used by `bidRoutes.ts`'s other endpoints. Added `server/test/tickets.test.ts`'s new `Sync guard against re-syncing after walk-ins` describe block (2 tests: blocked-and-named when a walk-in marker is present, proceeds normally when none is) - sanity-checked by neutralizing the marker check and confirming the block-case test failed, then reverted. Test count: 501 → 503; updated the count everywhere it's asserted. Doesn't touch Apps Script. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 503/503, `npm run build` clean.

- **Fixed the Bid-Management `Paid`/`Checked Out` column-comment mismatch flagged in `refactor-opportunities.md`.** The real `Bidder-Numbers` header (`Class_BidderManager.gs`'s `copyBiddersToBidderSheet()`, matching `syncService.ts`'s Node-side header exactly) is G=Checked Out, H=Paid - but `Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs`'s `recordPayment()` (all 3 branches, plus a stale commented-out draft) and `PayPalSweeper.gs`'s reconciliation/timeout paths all had their column comments backwards (`// Column G: Paid?`, `// Column H: Checked Out?`). This was cosmetic, not a live bug - every write site always sets both columns to the same value together, so nothing ever reads one independently of the other - but it was a landmine: the next person who trusted the comments and wrote code that touched only one of the two would have introduced a real one. Fixed by correcting every comment (8 sites total between the two files) to match the real header, rather than swapping the actual column writes - the writes were already going to the objectively correct columns for the header that's actually there; only the labels were wrong. Also fixed the same mislabeling where it had spread into test infrastructure and could have propagated the confusion further: `API_PAYPAL_TESTS.gs`'s manual sandbox-integration test (swapped its `isPaidField`/`isCheckedOutField` variable-to-column mapping), `GoogleAIStudioAndSweeper_UnitTests.gs` (its `GAS_TESTS_HEADER` fake header and 3 assertion messages), and the Node-side `server/test/gs-google-ai-studio.test.ts`/`gs-paypal-sweeper.test.ts` (both had a `HEADER` constant with the same G/H swap baked in, plus assertion comments repeating it - this is likely the original source the mismatch spread from into the `.gs` code's own comments). No production code paths changed - column indices in every `getRange(row, 7/8)` call are untouched, and no test's asserted boolean values changed, only which column each assertion/comment claims to be checking. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 503/503 (unchanged - no test assertions or fixtures' actual values were altered), `npm run build` clean. **Reference-copy comment fixes only - `Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs`, `PayPalSweeper.gs`, `API_PAYPAL_TESTS.gs`, and `GoogleAIStudioAndSweeper_UnitTests.gs` need to be manually copied into the live Bid-Management Apps Script editor for the comments to match there too (no functional change, so there's no urgency, but worth doing for consistency).**

- **Fixed the empty-state message on the Bid Management workflow's Bidders tab**, reported via a user bug report: with 0 bidders loaded, the table showed the confusing, unpolished `No bidders found matching ""` - the empty-state text always interpolated `searchTerm` regardless of whether a search was even active, so with the default empty string it rendered a bare, meaningless `""`. The user asked for this to mirror the sibling "Items" tab's empty state (`src/components/Items.tsx`), which already distinguishes "nothing has ever loaded" (`"No items loaded. Check your Spreadsheet ID in Settings."`) from "filtered down to nothing" (`"No items match your search."`). Bidders needed one more branch than Items: `useBiddersState.ts` tracks four independent filter dimensions (`searchTerm`, `statusFilter`, `paymentFilter`, `sourceFilter`), not just a single search term, so a new `hasActiveFilters` memo (`searchTerm !== "" || statusFilter !== "all" || paymentFilter !== "all" || sourceFilter !== "all"`) generalizes Items' single-field check to all four. `BiddersTable.tsx`'s `emptyMessage` now reads: `"No bidders loaded yet. Run \"Set Up Bid Management App\" or add a walk-in ticket to get started."` when `totalBiddersCount === 0` (a new prop - the raw, unfiltered bidder count from `useAuction()`, passed down through `Bidders.tsx`), `"No bidders match your search or filters."` when `hasActiveFilters` alone is true, and a defensive `"No bidders found."` fallback for the logically-unreachable case (bidders exist and no filter is active, so `sortedBidders` can never actually be empty there) - kept for type-safety/completeness, consistent with this codebase's general defensive-coding style. Added `Bidders.test.tsx`'s new `Bidders - Empty state messaging` describe block (2 tests): the "nothing loaded" message (and absence of both the old bare `""` text and the filtered-only message) with an empty `bidders`/`attendees` override, and the "filtered to nothing" message (and absence of the "nothing loaded" text) after typing a nonexistent name into the search box against the suite's normal non-empty fixture data. Sanity-checked by reverting `emptyMessage` back to the old `` `No bidders found matching "${""}"` `` shape and confirming both new tests failed with the real symptom, then restored and reconfirmed green. Doesn't touch Apps Script - this is a client-side-only display fix, no server or `.gs` involvement. Test count: 822 → 824 (395 backend unchanged, 429 frontend, up from 427); updated the count in `docs/agents/testing-batches.md`. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 824/824, `npm run build` clean.

- **Redesigned the Attendee List on the Auction Tickets workflow**, per the user-reported "Attendee List UI Layout Issues" bug report: the old `<table>`-based row packed four separate inline `EditableCell`s (first name, last name, email, phone) into one narrow column, with a per-field pencil icon each - the report specifically called out the "clunky" visual gap between the first/last name fields caused by that icon, on top of the overall table feeling out of step with the rest of the app's card-based screens. The user's own proposal (confirmed via a design mockup iterated live and approved before implementation) was to drop inline per-field editing entirely in favor of a single edit dialog. Rewrote `AttendeeRow.tsx` from a `<tr>`/`<td>` structure to a flexible row (name + source badge + guests-exceed-tickets warning, email/phone with icons, guest chips, a ticket-count stat, a one-click Paid/Pending payment pill, and hover-revealed Edit/Delete icon buttons) and replaced the `<table>`-based `AttendeesTable.tsx` with a new plain-div `AttendeesList.tsx` (loading skeleton and empty state reimplemented directly, since `DataTableWrapper.tsx` is `<table>`-specific and shared by Items/Bidders/Winners/Receipts - not worth generalizing for one screen). Added `EditAttendeeDialog.tsx`: one dialog covering contact fields, a card per guest (first/last/email, with a read-only "Own bidder #" badge where applicable - guest own-bidder-number was never editable even before this change), and a Paid/Pending toggle, all saved with one "Save Changes" click. `useTicketAttendeeState.ts` gained `attendeeToEdit`/`setAttendeeToEdit`/`isSavingEdit`/`handleEditSave` (mirroring the existing `attendeeToDelete` confirm-flow naming) - `handleEditSave` diffs the dialog's draft against the attendee being edited and only syncs fields that actually changed, by calling the existing, already-tested `updateField`/`updatePaymentStatus` per field/guest-field rather than introducing a new sync path, so their column-mapping and per-field optimistic-rollback behavior (guest column shift on the general "tickets" sheet included) is reused unchanged. The row's payment pill still updates instantly via `updatePaymentStatus` outside the dialog, matching the fast common case from before. Rewrote `AttendeeRow.test.tsx` for the new render-only row (the old file tested inline editing that no longer exists) and added `EditAttendeeDialog.test.tsx` (14 tests, real unmocked Dialog, matching `AddAttendeeDialog.test.tsx`'s convention) plus a `handleEditSave` describe block in `useTicketAttendeeState.test.ts` (6 tests: no-op with nothing being edited, only-changed-fields synced, guest field synced via its own column mapping, payment status synced via its own column, dialog closes with and without changes). Kept first/last name as two separate DOM text nodes (not one combined string) specifically so `TicketAttendeeTab.test.tsx`'s existing `getByText("Alice")`/`getByText("Bob")` search/filter assertions keep passing unchanged. Sanity-checked by running the new tests both before AND during the fix (they fail against the old inline-editing markup, as expected) - no separate revert-and-confirm pass, since these are new files replacing deleted ones rather than a small in-place fix. Test count: 824 → 845 (395 backend unchanged, 450 frontend, up from 429); updated the count in `docs/agents/testing-batches.md`. Doesn't touch Apps Script. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 845/845, `npm run build` clean.

- **Removed a documented, self-acknowledged duplication between GAS and Node for bidder-label generation** - Code Hardening item #5 from a `refactor-opportunities.md` audit pass over `scripts/GoogleAppsScriptsCode/TicketsSpreadsheet/`. `Class_BidderManager.gs`'s `getAllBidders()` re-derived every printed label's name/number by re-reading the Auction Tickets spreadsheet's staff/tickets/sponsor sheets and re-matching each person against the Bid-Management spreadsheet's `Bidder-Numbers` tab via a normalized name/email key - its own code comment admitted this "must stay byte-for-byte identical" to `server/services/syncService.ts`'s formatting logic, and this exact bug class had already bitten this codebase once (the guest-column-shift bug, which needed matching fixes in both `src/lib/attendeeParsing.ts` and this file). Eliminated the duplication by having Node read the already-built `Bidder-Numbers` rows directly instead of re-deriving them a second way: added `server/services/syncService.ts`'s `getBidderLabelList(client, spreadsheetId)`, which reads `Bidder-Numbers!A2:D` (guaranteed populated by the existing `ensureBidderNumbersSheet` call that already runs first) and returns a plain, number-sorted `{name, number}[]` list - no matching logic at all, since the ID and name are already sitting in the same row. `server/routes/productionRoutes.ts`'s `/tickets/bidder-labels` route now calls this and forwards the list as `apiCreateBidderLabels`'s second parameter, replacing the old `bidManagementSpreadsheetId` string (GAS no longer needs to open that spreadsheet itself). Rewrote `scripts/GoogleAppsScriptsCode/TicketsSpreadsheet/Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs`'s `createBidderLabels`/`apiCreateBidderLabels` to accept `bidders` directly, map each to a real `Bidder` instance (`Class_Bidder.gs`, unchanged), and hard-error on an empty/missing list before `BidderLabelsDoc` is ever constructed (matching the prior "validate before construct" ordering, just with a simpler check). Deleted `Class_BidderManager.gs` and its native-tier `BidderManager_UnitTests.gs` entirely - confirmed via grep that nothing else in the `.gs` tree referenced either - and updated `Class_BidderLabelsDoc.gs`'s stale docblock reference to the deleted class. Deleted the now-obsolete `server/test/gs-tickets-bidder-manager.test.ts` (tested the deleted class) and rewrote `server/test/gs-tickets-google-ai-studio.test.ts` for the new two-argument contract (empty/missing-bidders hard error, `apiCreateBidderLabels`'s error-conversion wrapper) - deliberately still not testing the full `BidderLabelsDoc`/`DocumentApp` pipeline, matching this test tier's existing scoping convention. Updated `server/test/production.test.ts`'s shared happy-path `expectedParams` for the new parameter shape and added a dedicated assertion to the existing "builds Bidder-Numbers first when it's missing" test confirming the freshly-computed rows flow straight through to the Apps Script call - this required fixing `server/test/handlers.ts`'s default `Bidder-Numbers` mock, which returned its header row for every range regardless of what was actually requested (harmless for every existing caller, which all requested header-inclusive ranges, but wrong for `getBidderLabelList`'s `A2:D`); it now only omits the header for a range that actually starts at row 2, matching real Sheets API behavior. Sanity-checked by temporarily disabling the new `getBidderLabelList` call in the route and confirming both the happy-path and dedicated assertions failed with the real symptom (an empty bidders array reaching the Apps Script call), then restored. No client-side changes needed - `TicketDocumentCreationTab.tsx` already sent `bidManagementSpreadsheetId` in the request body for Node's own use; the Apps Script call's parameter shape is an internal server/GAS contract the client never sees. Test count: 845 → 834 (384 backend, down from 395 net of the 9 deleted `gs-tickets-bidder-manager.test.ts` tests plus the smaller rewritten `gs-tickets-google-ai-studio.test.ts`; 450 frontend, unchanged); updated the count in `docs/agents/testing-batches.md`. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 834/834, `npm run build` clean. **Reference-copy change only - `Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs` and `Class_BidderLabelsDoc.gs` need to be manually copied into the live Tickets Apps Script editor, and `Class_BidderManager.gs`/`BidderManager_UnitTests.gs` manually deleted there too, before this fix takes effect on the live spreadsheet.**

- **Eliminated Bid Management's separate, drifting Items snapshot** - Code Hardening item #1 from `refactor-opportunities.md`. The Bid Management spreadsheet used to keep its own copy of the auction items (a sheet also named "Auction-Items", confusingly, living *inside* Bid Management) instead of always reading from the real single source of truth, the separate Auction Items spreadsheet. That copy was a one-time snapshot taken only when "Set Up Bid Management App" ran (`server/services/syncService.ts`'s old "Sync Step 7"), and lossy on top of being stale - reduced to 3 columns (ID, Item Name, Type) with an Active-only filter, so description/value/opening bid/min raise/donor/bundle info were gone even at the moment it was written. Concrete, observable bug this caused: staff recording a winning bid in `BidEntry.tsx` for an item added *after* the last sync got "Please fill in a valid item number" even though the item was real, because `BidEntry.tsx` looked the ID up against the stale copy. Fixed in four parts: (1) **Client** - `src/context/AuctionContext.tsx` now sources `items` via `useAuctionItems(config.auctionItemsSpreadsheetId)` (the same hook the top-level Auction Items workflow already used) instead of the deleted `useItemsData(bidManagementSpreadsheetId, config.auctionItemsSheet)`; since both hooks shared `src/lib/itemParsing.ts`'s `parseItemRows`, `Items.tsx`/`BidEntry.tsx`/`Dashboard.tsx`/`Checkout.tsx` needed no changes at all - they only ever consumed `useAuction().items: AuctionItem[]`. Deleted `src/hooks/useItemsData.ts` and retired the now-meaningless `config.auctionItemsSheet` setting (`src/types.ts`, `src/context/ConfigContext.tsx`, `src/hooks/useConfigSettingsState.ts`, `src/components/AuctionConfigurationCard.tsx`'s hidden field). (2) **Node email-receipt path** - `server/services/emailService.ts`'s `processEmail` gained an `itemsSpreadsheetId` parameter and now reads `'Auction-Items'!A:Z` from it instead of the Bid-Management `spreadsheetId`; threaded through `receiptService.ts`'s `processAllEmails`, `receiptRoutes.ts`'s `/email-all`/`/email-only`, and the client (`useCheckoutReceiptActions.ts`, `useCheckoutBatchActions.ts`, `Checkout.tsx` passing `config.auctionItemsSpreadsheetId`). The Google-Doc/PDF receipt path (`/generate`, `/generate-all`) needed no Node change - handled in GAS. (3) **GAS** - rewrote `scripts/GoogleAppsScriptsCode/BidManagementSpreadsheet/Class_AuctionItemManager.gs`'s `getItemName()` to open the master Items spreadsheet live via `this.config['auctionImportSheetID']` (already loaded from Bid Management's own `_Settings` tab - no new setting needed) instead of reading a local sheet, with an `overrides.itemsSpreadsheet` injection point mirroring `Class_BidderManager.gs`'s established cross-spreadsheet-read convention: throws a clear error on a missing ID or a failed/empty open (systemic misconfiguration), keeps the graceful `'Unknown Item'` fallback for one genuinely-unmatched ID. Deleted the now-fully-dead `ensureAuctionItemsSheet()`/`addStandardAuctionItems_()`/`removeExtraAuctionItemInfo_()` and the local-sheet `auctionItems` getter (confirmed unused elsewhere via grep), and removed `Class_GlobalVariables.gs`'s now-unused `auctionItemsString` constant. `Class_ReceiptManager.gs:116`'s `getItemName()` call site needed no changes. (4) **Stopped writing the drifting copy** - removed `performSheetSync`'s entire item-catalog-copy step (fuzzy header-mapping, Active-only filter, 11 hardcoded standard items, the `Auction-Items!A1` write) and its backup/recreate-sheet bootstrapping, dropping the now-unused `customAuctionImportSheetID` parameter (and `dataRoutes.ts`'s/`App.tsx`'s forwarding of it - `auctionImportSheetID` is still kept in sync in Bid Management's `_Settings` tab by `useConfigSettingsState.ts`'s existing `syncSettingToCloud` call, which is what GAS reads in part 3, just no longer *also* recomputed by this route). Removed `"Auction-Items"` from `auctionSetupService.ts`'s year-rollover sheet-deletion list, since the sheet is never created there anymore (a stale pre-fix copy is now simply left alone rather than actively cleaned up). Added `server/test/gs-bidmanagement-auction-item-manager.test.ts` (7 tests: live lookup, numeric-ID normalization, `'Unknown Item'` fallback, first-tab fallback, missing-ID throw, empty-cross-read throw, name-map caching) and a matching native-tier `scripts/GoogleAppsScriptsCode/BidManagementSpreadsheet/AuctionItemManager_UnitTests.gs` (`runAuctionItemManagerUnitTests`, smoke-verified via the same `vm`-sandbox loader before being committed as reference-only) - extended `server/test/helpers/fakeSheet.ts`'s `FakeSpreadsheet` with a `getSheets()` method to support the first-tab-fallback test. Sanity-checked the caching behavior specifically (the most subtle branch) by reverting the `if (!this._itemNameMap)` guard and confirming the cache test failed with the real symptom, then restored. Updated/removed fallout from removing the copy step: `server/test/items.test.ts`'s "Deleted items excluded from sync" test (tested the now-removed status-filter logic during sync, not actual item deletion), `server/test/auction-setup.test.ts`'s rollover-deletion test (now expects 2 deleted sheet IDs instead of 3, with a surviving leftover `Auction-Items` tab), and `server/test/bid-management.test.ts`'s two dead `"Auction-Items"` mock branches (confirmed unused - `recordWinningBid`/`checkoutMarkPaid` never read item details server-side at all). Test count: 834 → 840 (390 backend, up net of the new `gs-bidmanagement-auction-item-manager.test.ts` file's 7 tests minus the one removed `items.test.ts` test; 450 frontend, unchanged); updated the count in `docs/agents/testing-batches.md`. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 840/840, `npm run build` clean (server bundle shrank ~5kb from the removed sync logic). **Reference-copy change only - `Class_AuctionItemManager.gs` and `Class_GlobalVariables.gs` need to be manually copied into the live Bid Management Apps Script editor, and the new `AuctionItemManager_UnitTests.gs` added there too, before this fix takes effect on the live spreadsheet.**

- **Eliminated the third independent copy of "resolve a bidder's winning items and total"** - Code Hardening item #2 from `refactor-opportunities.md` (the `Class_ReceiptManager.gs`'s pure-data methods bullet). `findBidderColInBidSheet_` (find a bidder's column in the Bid-Management grid), `getEmailForBidder_` (look up their email from Bidder-Numbers), and `buildReceipt_` (read their winning items/prices/total) duplicated logic `server/services/emailService.ts`'s `processEmail` had already implemented independently for the emailed-receipt path - one in GAS (feeding the printed/PDF receipt and the PayPal invoice), one in Node, no shared source of truth. Scoped deliberately to just this: `getOrCreateInvoiceUrl`/`getPaymentLink` (real PayPal invoice creation) and `calculateAdjustedTotalWithFees` (calls `PayPalService.calculatePayPalSurcharge`, still duplicated with `financeUtils.ts`'s copy) stay in GAS for now - moving those depends on the separate, not-yet-done `Class_PayPalService.gs` migration bullet (porting PayPal's invoice CRUD to Node with its own credentials as env vars), which this fix does not touch. Extracted the shared logic into new `server/services/receiptDataService.ts`: `findBidderColumn`/`buildItemNameLookup`/`resolveWonItems` (the exact column-scan, fuzzy item-header-detection, and row-3+ item-loop `processEmail` already used, refactored out verbatim - confirmed behavior-preserving by running `emailService.test.ts` unchanged before and after, all 5 tests still pass byte-for-byte), plus new `buildBidderReceipt`/`buildAllBidderReceipts` which fetch Bid-Management/Bidder-Numbers/Items once and resolve one or every bidder's full receipt (name/email + items/total) - the batch version sorts ascending by last name to match `Class_BidderManager.gs`'s (now-removed) `getSortedBidderData_` exactly. `server/routes/receiptRoutes.ts`'s `/generate` and `/generate-all` (previously missing `spreadsheetId` destructuring entirely - a pre-existing, unrelated gap now closed as a side effect) now call these before invoking the Apps Script bridge, forwarding the finished receipt(s) as new parameters to `apiCreateIndividualReceipt`/`apiCreateAllReceipts` - the exact pattern established for bidder labels. Threaded `itemsSpreadsheetId` through `useCheckoutReceiptActions.ts`'s `handlePrintReceipt` and `useCheckoutBatchActions.ts`'s `startBatchPrint` (both already had it in scope from the Code Hardening item #1 fix, just weren't sending it to these two endpoints yet). On the GAS side: rewrote `Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs`'s `createIndividualReceipt`/`createAllReceipts` to accept the receipt data as parameters (with a `'No receipt data provided.'` hard error, validated before `ReceiptManager` is ever touched) instead of building it themselves; added `Class_Receipt.gs`'s `Receipt.fromData()` static factory to reconstruct a real `Receipt` instance from the plain object Node sends; rewrote `Class_ReceiptManager.gs` to remove the now-fully-dead `findBidderColInBidSheet_`/`getEmailForBidder_`/`buildReceipt_`/`bidManagement` getter, simplified `getPaymentLink` to just `return this.customPaymentUrl || "https://www.paypal.com"` (its old fallback branch - rebuilding a receipt via the methods just removed - was already unreachable in every real call path, both callers always set `customPaymentUrl` before any Doc-writing could reach it), and rewrote `processBatch_` to iterate the Node-supplied receipts array directly instead of the Bid-Management sheet. Also deleted `enableTestMode`/`sendAllEmailsOnly` (confirmed zero callers anywhere in the `.gs` tree, and both depended on the methods just removed) and, as a direct consequence, `Class_BidderManager.gs`'s now-fully-dead `bidders`/`biddersFullData` getters and `sortByLastName_`/`getSortedBidderData_` (their only callers were the deleted `sendAllEmailsOnly` and the old `createAllReceipts`, respectively - confirmed via grep before removing). Added `server/test/receiptDataService.test.ts` (11 tests: the pure column/lookup/item-resolution helpers, `buildBidderReceipt`'s happy path and all three error paths, `buildAllBidderReceipts`'s sort order and its three skip cases) and `server/test/gs-bidmanagement-receipt-manager.test.ts` (6 tests: `Receipt.fromData`'s reconstruction and its empty-array/zero-total defaults, `createIndividualReceipt`'s two validation paths verified to return before `Class_ReceiptManager.gs` is ever loaded, both bridge functions' error-conversion) - deliberately not testing the full Doc-generation/PayPal-invoice pipeline, since `ReceiptManager`'s constructor isn't designed for dependency injection (unconditionally constructs a real `AuctionItemManager`/`EmailManager`, which touch `SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet()`), matching the same scoping call already made for the Items-spreadsheet's Doc generators and the Tickets-spreadsheet's label doc. Updated `server/test/receipts.test.ts`'s three `/generate`/`/generate-all` happy-path tests (now require `spreadsheetId`/`itemsSpreadsheetId` and assert the Node-resolved receipt reaches the Apps Script call) and added two new "missing spreadsheetId" 400 tests to match the other receipt routes' existing convention. Sanity-checked by breaking `buildAllBidderReceipts`'s sort comparator and confirming the sort-order test failed with the real symptom, then restored; also caught and fixed a real bug in my own new `gs-bidmanagement-receipt-manager.test.ts` while writing it (constructing `GoogleAIStudio` with no overrides threw `SpreadsheetApp is not defined` before ever reaching the validation being tested - fixed by passing `{activeSpreadSheet: {}, config: {}}` overrides). Test count: 840 → 859 (409 backend, up 19 net: 11 new `receiptDataService.test.ts` + 6 new `gs-bidmanagement-receipt-manager.test.ts` + 2 new "missing spreadsheetId" tests; 450 frontend, unchanged); updated the count in `docs/agents/testing-batches.md`. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 859/859, `npm run build` clean (server bundle: 123.1kb → 127.5kb, the new shared receipt-resolution logic outweighing the deleted GAS-adjacent code it replaces). **Reference-copy change only - `Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs`, `Class_Receipt.gs`, `Class_ReceiptManager.gs`, and `Class_BidderManager.gs` need to be manually copied into the live Bid Management Apps Script editor before this fix takes effect there.**

### Removed

- Disabled `TicketsSpreadsheet/menu.gs`'s "Auction Documents" custom menu, per the user's request - the ORNS Auction Manager web app is now the single source of use for the Tickets spreadsheet too, matching the policy already applied to Bid-Management and Items. `onOpen`/`onInstall` are now documented no-ops. Confirmed via grep that `Class_SettingsManager.gs` and `Class_UIManager.gs` had no callers left anywhere in the folder once the menu was gutted (`SettingsManager` was only ever driven by the menu's `updateFileOrFolderLocations()` wrapper; `UIManager` was only ever used by `SettingsManager`) - deleted both files entirely, matching the outright-delete precedent from the Items-spreadsheet menu cleanup. Also removed `Class_BidderLabelsDoc.gs`'s global `generateBidderLabels()` wrapper function (added in the bidder-number fix above to interactively prompt for the Bid-Management spreadsheet ID) - it was menu-only and is now unreachable; the class's own `BidderLabelsDoc.prototype.generateBidderLabels()` *method* is untouched and still the one `Class_GoogleAIStudio.gs`'s live `createBidderLabels`/`apiCreateBidderLabels` path calls. Sanity-checked by loading the full remaining 11-file `.gs` folder into a sandboxed Node `vm` context in dependency order (all parse and resolve cleanly, file count matches disk exactly) and re-running `BidderManager_UnitTests.gs` against the real combined source (still 11/11). `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 503/503 (unaffected - nothing had test coverage on the deleted menu-only code), `npm run build` clean. **Reference-copy deletions only - this spreadsheet's manual menu was never live-critical to the app's real functionality, so nothing needs to change for correctness, though the user should copy the updated `menu.gs` (and remove the two deleted files) in the live Apps Script editor too, for consistency.**
- Deleted the two dead data-model classes and five menu-only orphaned manager classes flagged in `refactor-opportunities.md`, per the user's explicit go-ahead to delete outright. Confirmed each still had zero live callers before deleting (re-verified against the real code after the `BidManagementSpreadsheet/` move, not just trusted from the earlier finding): `Class_AuctionItem.gs` (`AuctionItem`) and `Class_Bidder.gs` (`Bidder`) - never instantiated anywhere; `Class_CheckInManager.gs`, `Class_CheckOutManager.gs`, `Class_BidEntryManager.gs`, `Class_AuctionSetupManager.gs`, `Class_SettingsManager.gs` - reachable only through `menu.gs`'s now-disabled `createCustomMenu()`, with no other live class depending on them (their shared dependencies - `BidderManager`, `AuctionItemManager`, `UIManager`, `GlobalStyles`, `DriveService`, `DocService` - all stay alive via `ReceiptManager`, which the app's real API bridge functions use). Also removed, as a direct consequence of that same investigation: `Class_ReceiptManager.gs`'s 4 menu-only wrapper functions (`createAllReceipts()`, `createIndividualReceipt()`, `testCreateIndividualReceipt()`, `testCreateAllReceipts()`) - `ReceiptManager` the class stays live (`GoogleAIStudio.createAllReceipts`/`.createIndividualReceipt` use a separate, parameterized path on it), only these 4 zero-arg menu entry points are gone; `Class_BidderManager.gs`'s `setupBiddersSheet()` one-line wrapper, discovered newly-dangling only after `menu.gs` itself was gutted (its own `copyBiddersToBidderSheet()` method it called stays, since `BidderManager` is alive elsewhere); and `menu.gs`'s `createCustomMenu()` function itself plus its `MENU_TITLE`/`PRE_AUCTION_TASKS`/`MID_AUCTION_TASKS`/`PRINTING_TASKS` constants - left in a prior pass as an inert "manual fallback," but with every manager class and wrapper function it referenced now gone, keeping it would have meant a dead function full of dangling string references to functions that no longer exist, which is strictly worse than not having it; `onOpen`/`onInstall` stay as documented no-ops. Logged one new follow-up in `refactor-opportunities.md`: `Class_ReceiptManager.gs`'s own `createIndividualReceipt()`/`createAllReceipts()` prototype methods (lines ~224/282) are now unreachable too (their only callers were the just-deleted global wrappers), but were deliberately left alone since they're substantial UI-prompt-driven methods, not one-liners, and weren't part of what was explicitly scoped for this deletion pass. `tsc --noEmit` clean; full suite 377/377 (unaffected - none of the deleted `.gs` code had Node-side test coverage), `npm run build` clean. **Reference-copy deletions only - these files were never live-critical (the manual menu they served was already disabled), so there's nothing that needs to be manually removed from the live Apps Script editor for correctness, though the user may want to delete the same files there too for consistency.**
- Obsolete pre-refactor docs describing a monolithic `server.ts` that no longer exists in this codebase: `BACKEND_DOCUMENTATION.md`, `CODE_AUDIT.md`, `ORPHANED_CODE_ANALYSIS.md`, `REFACTORING_ROADMAP.md`.
- Duplicate root-level `USER_GUIDE.md` (kept `docs/agents/USER_GUIDE.md`, the canonical location per `AGENTS.md`'s registry).
- Unused `firebase-blueprint.json` (its schema was accurate and was folded into `schema.md` first; `firestore.rules` and `firebase-applet-config.json` were kept since both are actually live-referenced).
- `src/components/AuctionAssistant.tsx`, the never-imported AI chat-assistant panel flagged in a prior audit as either needing to be wired in or deleted. Confirmed with the user this app doesn't need a chat panel - it needs AI-assisted auction-item name/description suggestions, which is a separate, already-live feature (`BundlingDialog.tsx` / `hooks/useBundling.ts`, backed by `/api/bundle/suggest-names` and `/api/bundle/suggest-description`) untouched by this deletion. Also removed its sole dependency, `react-markdown` (unused anywhere else in `src/`), from `package.json`. Left the backend `/api/assistant` route (`server/services/assistantService.ts`) alone rather than also deleting it - it's now orphaned (no remaining caller) but still has 5 passing tests in `server/test/bundle.test.ts`, and removing tested backend code wasn't part of what was asked; logged as a new follow-up in `refactor-opportunities.md` instead. Removing `react-markdown` and reinstalling surfaced that `@types/react`/`@types/react-dom` had never been explicit `package.json` devDependencies - the project was relying on them arriving as a transitive install via `react-markdown`'s own dependency tree, and dropping it pruned them, breaking `tsc --noEmit` across ~15 unrelated files (`Cannot find namespace 'React'`, and JSX `key` prop errors from the global JSX namespace disappearing). Fixed by adding explicit `@types/react`/`@types/react-dom` (`^19.0.1`, matching the pinned `react`/`react-dom` version) as real devDependencies, closing that latent fragility rather than just reverting. All tests pass unmodified; full suite 321/321, `npm run build` clean.
